PIT-SUN uses one empirical marginal table to define a bounded normal-score coordinate, its inverse-quantile lookup, a variance-controlled recovery base, and drift monitoring, then applies multiplicative SUN recovery to estimate the original-space expectation instead of directly inverting transformed predictions. Experiments on synthetic distributions, public benchmarks, large-scale industrial datasets, and online deployment show robust improvements in point accuracy, calibration, and ranking quality with lightweight deployment overhead.

</details>


### [56] [RhyMix: A Lightweight Adaptive Multi-Rhythm Network for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08234)
*Sumit Satishrao Shevtekar,Chandresh Kumar Maurya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RhyMix是一种混合神经网络架构，通过并行双路径建模（循环路径+多尺度时序卷积通道注意力路径）和自适应门控机制，在12个真实数据集中的10个上实现了长期时间序列预测的SOTA性能，模型仅约40K参数，推理延迟<5ms，适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测架构（Transformer、卷积、线性模型）均采用单一路径建模，无法同时捕捉短期波动、周期性季节循环、长期趋势和异常突变等多重时间模式，因此需要一种能自适应融合互补时序特征的混合架构。

Method: 提出RhyMix架构，包含两个并行编码分支：①循环路径（Cyclic Path），通过可学习循环嵌入引入显式季节归纳偏置；②多尺度时序卷积网络与通道注意力路径（MSTCN-CA Path），使用多尺度深度可分离膨胀卷积捕获不同感受野的时间依赖。核心创新是多层级自适应门控：路径门（Path Gate）为每个样本和通道动态组合四个预测头（Direct、趋势-季节分解、局部卷积、周期融合），混合门（Hybrid Gate）根据输入特征自适应平衡两条路径的贡献。

Result: 在12个真实数据集上进行长期预测基准测试，RhyMix在10个数据集上达到SOTA性能。模型仅约40K参数，计算复杂度在序列长度、通道数和预测视野上均为线性，推理延迟<5ms，适合资源受限的边缘设备和实时部署。

Conclusion: RhyMix通过并行双路径设计与多层级自适应门控机制，在保持轻量化和线性复杂度的同时，有效解决了单一架构无法兼顾多种时序模式的问题，为边缘端实时时序预测提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world time series exhibit complex dynamics characterized by multiple simultaneous temporal patterns: short-term fluctuations, periodic seasonal cycles, long-term trends, and irregular abrupt changes. However, many existing forecasting architectures rely on single-path temporal modeling--transformers capture long-range dependencies but smooth local variations, convolutions capture local patterns but have limited receptive fields, and linear models are efficient but cannot capture nonlinear dynamics. To address this, we introduce RhyMix (RHYthm MIXture), a hybrid neural architecture designed around a parallel dual-path modeling paradigm with adaptive gating mechanisms. RhyMix integrates two complementary encoding branches: (i) a Cyclic Path that incorporates explicit seasonal inductive bias through learnable cyclic embeddings, capturing predictable rhythmic patterns; and (ii) a lightweight Multi-Scale Temporal Convolutional Network with Channel Attention Path that employs multi-scale depthwise dilated convolutions to capture temporal dependencies across different receptive fields. A key innovation is the use of adaptive gating at multiple levels: a path gate dynamically combines four specialized forecasting heads (Direct, Trend-Seasonal Decomposition, Local Convolution, and Periodic Fusion) per sample and channel, while a hybrid gate adaptively balances the Cyclic and MSTCN-CA Paths based on input characteristics. This design ensures the model adapts to specific temporal patterns while maintaining linear complexity in sequence length, channels, and prediction horizon. Across extensive benchmarks on 12 real-world datasets for long-term forecasting, RhyMix achieves state-of-the-art performance on 10 of 12 datasets. The model remains lightweight (~40K params) with linear complexity and low-latency inference (<5ms),suitable for resource-constrained edge devices and real-time deployment.

</details>


### [57] [Structure Learning on Clustered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08238)
*Ryan Thompson,Matt P. Wand,Veerabhadran Baladandayuthapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在聚类数据中同时估计全局结构与局部聚类效应的因果结构学习方法，通过扩展混合模型的固定-随机效应框架，利用可微图耦合机制保证有向无环性，并提供了收敛性证明、可辨识性理论保证及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG结构学习方法假设总体同质，无法处理聚类数据中存在的聚类特异性（如患者特异性）变异，亟需一种能同时估计全局结构并建模局部聚类效应的方法。

Method: 将经典混合模型的固定-随机效应框架扩展到结构学习；提出可微图耦合机制确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环性；设计可证明收敛的一阶优化方法，并利用跨聚类的批量更新提高效率。

Result: 在合成与真实数据上，该方法能检测到被其他估计器遗漏的依赖关系；理论证明了模型可辨识性及结构渐近恢复性。

Conclusion: 该工作为聚类场景下的结构学习提供了兼具统计保证与计算效率的新范式，验证了其在发现聚类特异性因果结构方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Recent algorithmic advances have made directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning scalable for causal discovery. Yet, the currently available techniques assume a completely homogeneous population, precluding their application to clustered data where cluster-specific variations (e.g., patient-specific effects) are common. We address this issue by introducing a new approach that estimates a global structure while accounting for local cluster-level effects. The key idea is to extend the fixed- and random-effects framework of classical mixed models to the structure learning setting. Towards this end, we present a differentiable graph coupling mechanism that guarantees the union of the fixed- and random-effects graphs remains acyclic. Computationally, we provide a provably convergent first-order method and leverage efficient batched updates across clusters. Statistically, we establish identifiability of the model and show that our approach recovers the true structure asymptotically. In experiments on real and synthetic data, our proposal detects dependencies missed by alternative estimators, underscoring its value for structure learning in clustered settings.

</details>


### [58] [An interpretable Good--Turing restart criterion for k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08243)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对k-means++算法中人为固定重启次数的问题，提出了一种基于Good-Turing估计的自适应重启准则GTRC，能够在数据难度变化时动态决定何时停止重启，实验表明该方法在36个数据集上达到了与精心选取的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数会根据数据难度自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means++算法通常需要多次重启以避免局部最优，但重启次数的选择几乎是任意且对所有数据集统一使用，这导致在简单数据集上浪费计算资源，在困难数据集上可能重启不足，且影响了基于此选择的比较的公平性。

Method: 提出了GTRC（Good-Turing重启准则），它结合了Good-Turing估计、一个经过证明的无条件界以及一个基于置信度的界，用于估计继续重启能改善当前结果的概率，当该概率低于用户指定的容忍度ε时停止重启。

Result: 在36个数据集上，GTRC达到了与精心选取的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数会根据数据集难度自适应变化，且这种变化由可解释的、数据依赖的信号驱动，而非固定规则。

Conclusion: GTRC为固定k-means++重启次数提供了一种有理论依据且可报告的替代方案，能够根据数据难度自适应调整重启次数，避免了人为选择的随意性。

Abstract: The k-means++ algorithm is commonly restarted multiple times to avoid poor local optima, yet the number of restarts is almost always chosen arbitrarily and applied uniformly regardless of data set difficulty. This undermines any comparison relying on such a choice and wastes computation on easy data sets while potentially under-serving hard ones. We introduce GTRC, a restart criterion combining a Good-Turing estimate, a proven unconditional bound, and a confidence-based bound on the probability that a further restart would improve on the current result, stopping once this probability falls below a user-specified tolerance $\varepsilon$. Across 36 data sets, GTRC reached clustering quality competitive with well-chosen fixed restart counts, while the number of restarts used varied considerably and appropriately with data set difficulty, governed by an interpretable, data-dependent signal rather than a fixed rule. GTRC offers a principled and reportable alternative to fixing the number of $k$-means++ restarts in advance. Software:https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion.

</details>


### [59] [CASL-VAE: Learning Structured Latent Variables from Unpaired Data for Semi-supervised Clustering and Paired Sample Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08254)
*Sai Spandana Chintapalli,Pratik Chaudhari,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASL-VAE，一种深度对比潜在变量模型，能在无配对数据的情况下学习结构化潜在生成因子，将变异分解为连续公共因子和分层显著因子（离散亚型+连续亚型内变异），用于异质性分析和跨域生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在无配对数据且存在异质性目标变异时，难以分离目标群体中的多模态特异性变异模式。

Method: 提出CASL-VAE，将变异分解为跨群体共享的连续公共潜在因子和分层显著潜在因子（离散亚型+连续亚型内变异），通过变分推断利用无配对数据近似联合似然优化。

Result: 在半合成神经影像数据上亚型恢复和配对样本生成优于基线聚类和生成模型，在阿尔茨海默病数据上揭示了生物学合理的异质性。

Conclusion: CASL-VAE有效解决了无配对数据下的异质性分离问题，为配对样本生成和跨域分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Quantifying variability in a target population relative to a reference population is central to many scientific and clinical problems (e.g., diseased vs. healthy). Yet, without paired data and in the presence of heterogeneous target variation, existing methods struggle to separate multiple modes of target-specific variation. We propose \textit{CASL-VAE}, a deep contrastive latent variable model that learns structured latent generative factors from unpaired data. CASL-VAE factorizes variation into continuous common latent factors shared across populations and hierarchical salient latent factors that model target-specific heterogeneity as discrete subtypes and continuous within-subtype variation. Using variational inference, we show how approximate joint likelihood optimization over reference and target domains can be performed using unpaired data, providing a principled basis for paired-sample generation and cross-domain analysis. We validate CASL-VAE on semi-synthetic neuroimaging data, demonstrating improved subtype recovery and paired-sample generation compared to baseline clustering and generative models. We also validate its ability to reveal biologically plausible heterogeneity in Alzheimer's disease.

</details>


### [60] [Classifier Chain-based Pathological Test Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08299)
*Abu Rafe Md Jamil,Nayan Malakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分类器链（CC）的病理性检测推荐系统，利用患者症状在医生会诊前进行多标签检测推荐。在自建数据集上，逻辑回归+CC模型准确率达98.83%，集成模型最优F1-score为0.89，并通过SHAP可解释性分析验证了模型与医学知识的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诊断过程中检测推荐延迟且依赖医生主观判断，影响诊疗效率和质量。本研究旨在通过机器学习方法，在医生会诊前基于患者症状自动推荐病理检测，加速检测选择过程。

Method: 将检测推荐构建为多标签分类问题，采用分类器链（CC）技术建模检测项目间的依赖关系。收集SOUTHERN.IML病理数据并构建自定义数据集，比较逻辑回归、决策树、随机森林等多种算法，使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 逻辑回归+CC模型整体准确率最高达98.83%；多数投票集成模型在平衡性上最优，精确率0.93、召回率0.85、F1-score 0.89。SHAP分析显示模型诊断推理与已有医学知识一致。

Conclusion: 分类器链技术能提升传统算法在诊断过程中的效率，提供准确的检测推荐，且模型具备临床可解释性，有助于医生在关键场景中做出合理决策。

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnoses are essential for quality patient care. However, delayed recommendation of diagnostic tests and physicians' subjective interpretations can hinder effective care. This study introduces a pathological test recommendation system that speeds up the test selection process using patient symptoms before physician consultation. The recommendation task is framed as a multi-label classification problem utilising the Classifier Chain (CC) technique to consider dependencies between tests. We collected data from the SOUTHERN.IML pathology and then created a custom dataset with the help of the expertise. Multiple machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were applied to compare models and identify the best fit for our study context. The Logistic Regression with CC model had the highest overall accuracy at 98.83%, while the Majority Voting ensemble model provided the best balance with a precision of 0.93, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.89. To ensure transparency of the models and clinical interpretability, we used Explainable AI (XAI) techniques utilising SHAP (SHapley Additive Explanations), which identifies how each symptom is contributing to a test recommendation. The diagnostic reasoning revealed by the model was consistent with established medical knowledge of symptoms for the recommended tests, which further adds confidence to the model's reliability for diagnostic purposes. The reasoning could help physicians make logical decisions in critical scenarios. Overall, our findings suggest that CC can improve the efficiency of the traditional algorithms in diagnostic process providing accurate test recommendations.

</details>


### [61] [Learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under Locally Sampleable Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08303)
*Weiming Feng,Xiongxin Yang,Yixiao Yu,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在具有高效局部采样器的图模型下学习AC0电路的拟多项式时间算法，通过模拟和截断经典Glauber动力学建立新的低度近似，突破了先前工作中对多项式增长条件的要求，并应用于硬核模型和伊辛模型等二自旋系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展AC0电路在相关分布下的学习保证是计算学习理论中一个长期存在的挑战。先前工作虽将均匀分布下的结果推广至具有强空间混合和多项式增长的吉布斯分布，但多项式增长条件限制了其适用范围。本文旨在去除这一限制，将学习保证扩展到更广泛的图模型。

Method: 通过模拟并适当截断经典Glauber动力学，建立吉布斯分布的一种新的低度近似。以此为核心工具，设计拟多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 在任意有界度图上的二自旋系统（包括硬核模型和伊辛模型）中，在接近各自采样阈值的参数区域内，实现了AC0电路的拟多项式时间学习。

Conclusion: 本文通过绕过多项式增长条件，将AC0电路在相关分布下的学习保证推广到更一般的图模型，为处理更广泛的相关分布学习问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: The problem of learning constant-depth circuits holds profound implications for computational learning theory. In a seminal result, by introducing the low-degree algorithm, Linial, Mansour, and Nisan (J. ACM 1993) presented a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under the uniform distribution. However, obtaining comparable learning guarantees for broader classes of correlated distributions has remained a longstanding challenge. Recently, Chandrasekaran, Gaitonde, Moitra, and Vasilyan (arXiv 2026) extended these guarantees to Gibbs distributions on bounded-degree graphical models with both strong spatial mixing and polynomial growth.
  In this paper, we give a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under graphical models that admit efficient local samplers, circumventing the polynomial-growth requirement in prior work. The key ingredient is a new low-degree approximation for Gibbs distributions, established by simulating and suitably truncating the classical Glauber dynamics. As applications, this framework yields learners for two-spin systems, including the hard-core model and Ising model, on arbitrary bounded-degree graphs, in regimes approaching their respective sampling thresholds.

</details>


### [62] [Write-Protected Discrete Bottlenecks for Language-Grounded World Models: A Structural Limitation and Sufficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08312)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了语言梯度直接注入离散符号瓶颈（Gumbel-Softmax）会导致符号坍塌（2.2/64 tokens）或语义学习失败（准确率≤9.2%），并提出三层次解决方案：切断梯度链、引入无参数记忆表进行共现计数、使用DP-Means流聚类处理符号碰撞，在32个种子中实现零符号坍塌，语义绑定准确率达97.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端范式（RT-2、Octo、PaLM-E）默认语言梯度可以直接塑造物理符号表征，作者质疑这一假设的安全性，并发现其确实存在结构性失败问题。

Method: 首先通过实验发现GumbelBottleneck中语言梯度导致符号坍塌或语义学习失败；然后提出三约束条件：(1) z.detach()切断梯度链；(2) 非参数记忆表（Dict[symbol→Counter[label]]）进行零梯度共现绑定；(3) DP-Means流聚类自动子簇分裂处理符号碰撞。

Result: 74次独立实验中，32个种子均实现零符号坍塌；语义绑定准确率97.2%（vs 无Layer3的22.2%）；跨CNN、V-JEPA 300M、CLIP ViT-L三种编码架构，两个环境，三种纹理条件均达到79-100%语义绑定；训练参数少于2M，无需LLM微调。

Conclusion: 在GumbelBottleneck家族内部，语言梯度与离散符号瓶颈的冲突是结构性的而非优化问题；所提出的三层次约束条件足以根本性地防止该失败。

Abstract: How should language interface with a world model's discrete symbol system? The dominant paradigm -- end-to-end injection of LLM/VLM features into robot world models (RT-2, Octo, PaLM-E) -- implicitly assumes that language gradients can directly shape physical symbol representations. We ask whether this assumption is safe, find that it is not, and characterize the minimal architectural constraint that prevents the failure. Any language gradient entering a Gumbel-softmax-based discrete symbol bottleneck forces a structural trade-off: the vanilla estimator collapses to 2.2/64 symbols (4/5 seeds), while five anti-collapse strategies maintain diversity but fail to learn semantic labels (all <= 9.2% accuracy). No tested GumbelBottleneck variant achieves both objectives simultaneously. Within this family of discrete bottlenecks, the failure is structural rather than a matter of optimization. We characterize a sufficient set of three constraints that prevent the failure: (1) cut the gradient chain (z.detach()), preventing language signals from reaching the symbol bottleneck; (2) provide a gradient-free semantic channel -- a non-parametric Memory Table (Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]], zero parameters, zero gradients) where co-occurrence counting replaces gradient-based binding; (3) handle symbol collisions via DP-Means streaming clustering for automatic sub-cluster splitting. All three layers together achieve 97.2% grounding accuracy vs. 22.2% without Layer 3. Across two experiments spanning 74 independent runs, we demonstrate zero symbol collapse in all 32 seeds, with the blackboard achieving 79-100% semantic binding across three encoder architectures (CNN, V-JEPA 300M, CLIP ViT-L), two environments, and three texture conditions. The fix trains fewer than 2M parameters and requires no LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [63] [ArtMine: Discovering and Formalizing Artistic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08331)
*Kaustubh Kumar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Blessin Varkey,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtMine框架，通过整合碎片化历史证据，利用皮尔士溯因推理推断艺术创作过程，生成可解释的流程图谱，实现从最终作品到创作过程的计算建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI仅能建模成品艺术品的分布，无法捕捉创作过程中的迭代决策、材料操作与情境影响，而艺术创作过程仅以碎片化方式记录，缺乏计算化形式化方法。

Method: 将异质历史证据整合为结构化知识库，利用皮尔士溯因推理代理推断基于证据的创作步骤，将步骤转化为组合图与渲染提示，通过自我反思优化生成与参考作品之间的偏差。

Result: 基于跨艺术家与艺术运动的多源历史数据的概念验证案例表明，碎片化文献证据能够支持连贯、可解释且可审计的艺术创作流程表征。

Conclusion: 该工作从建模最终作品转向建模创作过程，推动了以过程为中心的人机协同创意系统发展，可支持艺术解读、创意教育、反思协作与文化生产的计算研究。

Abstract: Understanding how artworks are created requires reasoning about the iterative decisions, material operations, and contextual influences that shape artistic production. While recent generative AI systems can synthesize artworks with high fidelity, they primarily model distributions over finished artifacts rather than the creative processes underlying their creation. In practice, artistic workflows are only partially documented through fragmented sources such as archival records, preparatory studies, correspondence, etc., making process-level understanding difficult to formalize computationally. In this work, we introduce ArtMine, a framework for discovering and formalizing artistic processes from heterogeneous historical evidence. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous artwork evidence into a structured repository, from which a Peircean abductive agent infers evidence-grounded production steps. These steps are converted into a compositional graph and rendering prompt, then optimized through self-reflection over deviations between the generated and reference artworks. We provide a preliminary proof-of-concept case study using open-domain historical sources across multiple artists and artistic movements, demonstrating that fragmented documentary evidence can support coherent, interpretable, and auditable representations of artistic workflows. By modeling creative processes rather than only final artifacts, our work moves toward process-centred human-AI co-creativity systems that can support artistic interpretation, creative education, reflective collaboration, and computational studies of cultural production.

</details>


### [64] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段扩散模型遗忘框架，通过自动合成流形近端锚点实现稳定遗忘，解决现有方法中因缺乏流形近端锚点导致的偏差和不鲁棒问题，并设计交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近性的高效替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散遗忘方法存在两大问题：基于锚点的方法依赖人工语义选择锚点，易导致有偏遗忘；无锚点方法因潜在更新无约束而固有不鲁棒。本文在流形假设下理论证明了缺乏流形近端锚点会诱发显著的法向空间漂移，损害遗忘性能。

Method: 提出两阶段框架，自动合成流形近端锚点；引入交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近性的高效替代，避免直接几何流形优化的计算不可行性。

Result: 在多种基线方法上实现鲁棒无偏遗忘，目标概念移除提升31.04% (CLIP分数)，非目标效用提升4.18%；可集成到现有方法中，概念移除平均提升6.30%，效用平均提升6.65%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了扩散模型遗忘中的不稳定问题，兼具鲁棒性与无偏性，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

</details>


### [65] [Spectral Analysis of Dueling Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08340)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对去噪Q-learning（Dueling Q-learning）进行了理论分析，建立了确定性版本下的精确切换线性系统表示，以及随机采样版本下的有限时间期望误差界，揭示了值函数与优势函数更新对Q函数不同分量的增益作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪Q-learning虽在实践中表现出色，但其理论理解仍有限。现有分析仅针对正则化形式，而纯表格更新的收敛性尚未被充分理解。

Method: 对确定性去噪Q-learning导出精确的切换线性系统表示；对随机采样版本推导有限时间期望误差界。

Result: 建立了未正则化、未投影的常数步长去噪Q-learning递归的收敛保证，阐明了值和优势更新分别以不同增益作用于Q函数的动作公共分量（值函数）和动作差分分量（优势函数）。

Conclusion: 给出了表格去噪Q-learning更为完整的理论保证，增进了对算法内部机制的深入理解。

Abstract: Q-learning is a fundamental algorithm in reinforcement learning (RL) for solving discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) when the transition kernel is unknown. The deep Q-network (DQN) extends Q-learning by using a deep neural network for Q-function approximation, which makes Q-learning applicable to more practical high-dimensional problems. Dueling Q-learning decomposes the Q-function into a value function and an advantage function and learns the two components jointly, which can improve learning efficiency. However, the theoretical understanding of dueling Q-learning is still limited. Recent work has initiated an analysis of tabular dueling Q-learning, but existing guarantees focus on a regularized formulation and leave the pure tabular update less completely understood. This paper strengthens that line of analysis by adding a direct interpretation of the centered tabular decomposition and by establishing convergence guarantees for the unregularized, unprojected constant step-size recursion. In particular, we derive an exact switching linear system representation for deterministic dueling Q-learning and a finite-time error bound in expectation for the sampled stochastic version. The analysis clarifies how the value and advantage updates act as different gains on the action-common (value function) and action-differential (advantage function) components of the Q-function.

</details>


### [66] [Certified Interventional Fidelity: Anytime-Valid, Adaptive Evaluation of Causal Claims in Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08349)
*Amir Asiaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF)，一个为可解释性干预评估提供统计保障的框架，通过将评估量形式化为因果估计量并给出置信区间/置信序列，包括在自适应采样下的有效性保证，实验表明方差自适应的投注序列可将认证成本降低10-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性研究的干预实验（如激活修补、消融等）通常只报告点估计，忽略了有限采样和评估选择带来的不确定性，导致难以区分报告的 fidelity 或修补效果是稳定的因果主张还是抽样噪声的产物。

Method: CIF 首先将待报告的量形式化为因果估计量（在指定输入分布和干预分布下边界得分的期望），然后提供置信区间和任意有效的置信序列（anytime-valid confidence sequences），并引入有界混合重要性加权（bounded mixture importance weighting）以支持自适应干预采样下的统计推断。实例化包括 Hoeffding 风格序列和方差自适应投注序列（variance-adaptive betting sequences）。

Result: 在 MNIST 抽象和 GPT-2 Small IOI 电路上的实验表明，CIF 能够认证高 fidelity 声明，揭示方法间表面差异缺乏统计支持，并使干预分布的敏感性显式化。方差自适应投注序列相比 Hoeffding 序列将认证成本降低10-30倍。

Conclusion: CIF 为可解释性干预评估提供了必要的统计基础设施，使因果声明具有可靠的置信度保证，并暴露了当前评估中隐式的分布选择敏感性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often evaluates explanations by intervening on a model: swapping hidden states, patching activations, ablating components, or comparing a compressed model to the original one. These experiments are usually summarized by a point estimate, even though the evaluation may be monitored while it runs or adapted toward suspected failures. This makes it hard to tell whether a reported fidelity or patching effect is a stable causal claim or a consequence of finite sampling and evaluation choices. We introduce Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF), a statistical layer for interventional interpretability evaluations. CIF first writes the quantity being reported as a causal estimand: an expectation of a bounded score over a stated input distribution and a stated intervention distribution. It then provides confidence intervals and anytime-valid confidence sequences for this estimand, including under adaptive intervention sampling via bounded mixture importance weighting. We instantiate CIF with Hoeffding-style sequences and variance-adaptive betting sequences, the latter reducing certification cost by 10-30x in our experiments. On MNIST abstractions and GPT-2 Small IOI circuits, CIF certifies high-fidelity claims, shows when apparent method differences are not statistically supported, and makes sensitivity to the intervention distribution explicit.

</details>


### [67] [Self-Adaptive Anomaly Detection with Reinforcement Learning and Human Feedback in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08373)
*Matthias Weiß,Athreya Hosahalli Prakash,Maurice Artelt,Falk Dettinger,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向联网车辆自动驾驶系统的在线异常检测框架，集成了自适应检测器选择、统计漂移检测和人在环重训练三种机制，在微服务架构中实现了持续自适应监控，实验表明F1分数显著优于单一检测器，且能适应软件更新引发的概念漂移而不产生灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网车辆作为自主信息物理系统在运行中持续演化（如OTA更新、配置变更、工作负载变化），导致静态诊断方法性能无声退化。现有方法未能将模型自适应与操作员集成整合为统一的监控闭环。

Method: 1) 基于自注意力机制的分层深度Q网络从候选池中为每个微服务选择最优检测器，利用服务间依赖关系；2) 三种统计漂移检测器集成，三判一致才触发告警，优先保证精确率；3) 人在环重训练机制，采用待定转移缓冲区和60/40优先级回放策略，在融入专家知识的同时保留历史学习结果。

Result: 在自动代客泊车应用场景的7个后端微服务测试床上，注意力增强智能体的F1分数达到0.69，而任何单一检测器统一应用最高仅0.11。软件更新引发概念漂移后F1降至0.52，经操作员触发的重训练后恢复至0.65（新分布），同时保持0.69（旧分布）。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了在演化系统中的持续自适应异常检测，能够在不发生灾难性遗忘的前提下兼顾新旧数据分布，验证了检测器选择、漂移检测与人在环重训练三者协同的有效性。

Abstract: Connected vehicles are autonomous cyber-physical systems whose behavior must be continuously monitored during operation to detect deviations from normal operation before they propagate into failures. Such evaluation is challenging because the systems themselves evolve: over-the-air updates, configuration changes, and shifting workloads alter the definition of normal behavior, causing static diagnostic methods to degrade silently over time. Existing approaches typically address either automated model adaptation or operator integration in isolation, rather than as a single coordinated supervisory loop.
  This paper presents an online anomaly detection framework for autonomous CPS that integrates three coordinated mechanisms. A factorized deep Q-network with self-attention selects the most suitable detector from a candidate pool for each monitored service, exploiting inter-service dependencies in the microservice topology. An ensemble of three statistical drift detectors monitors the input distribution and raises an alarm only when all three concur, prioritizing precision over recall. A human-in-the-loop retraining mechanism, built around a pending transition buffer and a 60/40 prioritized replay strategy, allows the operator to incorporate expert knowledge while preserving the system's learned response to prior data distributions.
  The framework is evaluated on a connected-vehicle testbed running an automated valet parking application across seven backend microservices. The attention-augmented agent achieves an F1 score of 0.69, compared to at most 0.11 for any single detector applied uniformly. Following a real software update that induces measurable concept drift, F1 drops to 0.52; after operator-triggered retraining, performance recovers to 0.65 on the new distribution while remaining at 0.69 on the prior one, demonstrating sustained adaptation without catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [68] [Eigenvalue Calibration for Semantic Embeddings of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08377)
*Sebastian G. Gruber,Nassim Walha,Francis Bach,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种校准大语言模型语义嵌入特征值的框架，通过将LLM与语义嵌入解释为密度矩阵预测器，并应用温度缩放对特征值进行校准，建立了熵-风险等价性，推导了特征值校准不等式，证明了温度缩放特征值在最小化适当评分风险时的最优校准性。实验表明当前LLM系统性地过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义嵌入特征值的不确定性量化方法缺乏校准理论基础，分类概率的校准结果无法直接迁移至特征值，亟需建立专门针对特征值的校准框架。

Method: 将LLM及其生成答案的语义嵌入视为密度矩阵预测器，提出对特征值进行温度缩放校准；建立熵-风险等价性，推导特征值中心校准不等式，证明温度缩放特征值在最小化适当评分风险时实现最优校准。

Result: 实验表明当前LLM系统性地过度自信，理论结果验证了所提校准框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了语义嵌入不确定性量化的理论基础与实践方法，填补了特征值校准的理论空白。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs), and eigenvalues of semantic embeddings have recently emerged as a key tool in state-of-the-art methods. However, conventional calibration results developed for classification probabilities cannot be directly transferred to eigenvalues. We address this gap by proposing a novel framework for calibrating the eigenvalues of semantic embeddings. We interpret LLMs combined with semantic embeddings of their generated answers as density matrix predictors, and we propose a novel approach to calibrate density matrix predictors by applying temperature scaling to their eigenvalues. We establish entropy-risk equivalence under calibration, derive a central calibration inequality specific to eigenvalues, and prove that temperature-scaled eigenvalues optimize calibration when minimizing proper score risks. Experiments on a variety of real-world settings show that current LLMs are systematically overconfident, and validate our theoretical findings. Together, these results advance the foundations and practice of uncertainty quantification for semantic embeddings.

</details>


### [69] [Dynamics of Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Near a Manifold of Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08380)
*Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了梯度下降法中大步长理论，从标量输出过参数化最小二乘推广到向量输出过参数化最小二乘以及平坦极小值流形邻域，通过解决奇异偏微分方程等挑战，建立了规范形式与三个收敛定理，并应用于深度矩阵分解，证明了平坦极小值集构成球面乘积上的纤维丛且锐度是Morse-Bott函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典梯度下降理论要求步长小于锐度倒数的两倍，但深度神经网络训练中常违反此条件。近期工作在单标量输出的过参数化最小二乘中建立了大步长GD的规范形式与收敛结果，本文将其推广到更一般的向量输出场景和平坦极小值流形邻域。

Method: (1) 将规范形式与三个收敛定理从标量输出推广到向量输出的过参数化最小二乘；(2) 从孤立平坦极小值推广到平坦极小值流形邻域；(3) 通过一种新颖方法解决奇异偏微分方程；(4) 将框架应用于深度矩阵分解。

Result: (1) 建立了向量输出过参数化最小二乘的大步长GD规范形式与三个收敛定理；(2) 证明了平坦极小值集构成球面乘积上的纤维丛；(3) 证明了锐度沿该流形是Morse-Bott函数。

Conclusion: 本文成功将大步长GD理论从标量输出推广到向量输出及平坦极小值流形邻域，所提出的解奇异PDE方法具有独立价值，且深度矩阵分解的应用验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: An important quantity in the theory of gradient descent (GD) is the \emph{sharpness}, defined as the largest eigenvalue of the objective Hessian. Classical analyses typically require the step size to be uniformly smaller than twice the reciprocal of the sharpness, but this condition is frequently violated in the training of deep neural networks. Recent work bridges this gap in the setting of overparametrised least-squares with a \emph{single scalar output}, providing a normal form for large-step GD in a neighbourhood of an \emph{isolated} flat minimum and establishing three corresponding convergence results. In this paper, we extend this theory in two directions: (1) to overparametrised least-squares with \emph{vector-valued outputs} (including regression with arbitrarily many observations), and (2) to a neighbourhood of a \emph{manifold} of flat minima (which we show is essential for applications such as matrix factorisation). We generalise both the normal form and all three convergence theorems of \cite{macdonaldeos} to this broader setting, overcoming several technical challenges, including the solution of a singular partial differential equation via a novel method that may be of independent interest. We further show that our framework applies to deep matrix factorisation under mild assumptions, yielding several new structural results. In particular, we prove that the set of flat minima forms a fibre bundle over a product of spheres, and that the sharpness is Morse-Bott along this manifold.

</details>


### [70] [Predicting Male Fertility Using Machine Learning: A Semen Parameters Based Analysis with the VISEM Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08429)
*Shahnawaz Qureshi,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Fozan,Emal Kawal,Syed Aziz Shah,Sattam Al-Anazi,Syed MuhammadZeeshan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用VISEM数据集和LazyPredict框架测试了40多种机器学习算法，其中Nearest Centroid分类器以94.2%的准确率在基于精液参数（浓度、活力、形态）的男性生育力分类中表现最优，验证了机器学习在男性不育诊断中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是常被忽视的生殖健康问题，传统精液分析在临床评估中存在局限性，亟需更客观、准确的自动化诊断工具来辅助临床决策。

Method: 采用VISEM数据集（85名参与者，三类生育状态），经预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架训练并评估40余种分类模型，以5折交叉验证和多元ROC-AUC分析验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: Nearest Centroid分类器准确率达94.2%，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等其他模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型能够提供快速、准确、客观的精液质量评估，有望辅助男科及辅助生殖技术中的临床决策，推动生育力诊断的智能化发展。

Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

</details>


### [71] [Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality Transportation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08475)
*Jiewen Deng,Hangchen Liu,Junchen Li,Boyuan Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreMo是一种轻量级的频域多模态交通预测框架，通过模态级频谱滤波（MFF）和频率引导的协同融合（FSI），显式利用频域特性实现跨模态自适应协同，在真实数据集上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态交通预测面临两大挑战：(1)不同模态具有不同的频谱特性，(2)模态间在不同频率上存在不均匀交互，而现有方法主要在时域操作或依赖粗粒度特征融合，无法有效处理频域差异。

Method: 提出FreMo框架，包含两个核心模块：(1)模态级频率滤波器(MFF)，自适应地精炼每个模态内的频谱成分，增强信息频率并抑制噪声；(2)频率引导的协同融合器(FSI)，基于各频率下各模态的相对贡献选择性聚合跨模态信息。该框架支持即插即用，可与通用时间序列骨干网络集成。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多种预测场景下持续优于最先进的基线方法，具有优越的性能和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模频域特性并实现自适应选择性跨模态协同，是提升多模态交通预测性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-modality transportation refers to urban systems composed of multiple transportation modes, such as traffic flow and public transit, whose dynamics are coupled by shared temporal patterns. Accurate multi-modality transportation forecasting remains challenging because (1) different modalities exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and (2) interact unevenly across frequencies, whereas most existing methods operate primarily in the time domain or rely on coarse feature fusion. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight yet effective Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality modeling (FreMo) that explicitly exploits the frequency domain to enable adaptive and selective cross-modality synergy. FreMo disentangles modality-wise spectral refinement from cross-modality synergy and supports plug-and-play integration with general time series backbones. Specifically, FreMo introduces a Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF) to adaptively refine spectral components within each modality, emphasizing informative frequencies while suppressing noise. FreMo further incorporates a Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI) that selectively aggregates information across modalities based on their relative contribution at each frequency, facilitating effective cross-modality knowledge sharing while mitigating negative transfer. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FreMo consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with superior performance and generalization across diverse forecasting scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo.

</details>


### [72] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了30种代表性神经网络架构在CIFAR-10上使用25种学习率调度策略的效果，测试了3,938个模型变体，发现调度器选择高度依赖架构，CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR普遍优于基本衰减策略，最佳配置达到86.45%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高且难以穷举，传统AutoML方法通常将其视为次要超参数，缺乏对其影响的系统研究。

Method: 从LEMUR数据集中选取30种代表性架构（卷积和Transformer系列），通过自动化源代码注入应用9个PyTorch调度器家族的25种配置，在CIFAR-10上评估了3,938个模型变体。

Result: 最佳配置top-1准确率86.45%，237个变体超过80%；CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR一致优于基本衰减策略；调度器选择高度依赖架构。

Conclusion: 学习率调度器与架构存在强依赖关系，研究结果已被收录至LEMUR nn-dataset，为原则性的调度器选择提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

</details>


### [73] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，将工业系统异常检测重新定义为通过外生变量持续验证格兰杰因果一致性，利用多尺度对齐和梯度基矩阵捕捉因果关系破坏，在真实工业数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法大多聚焦于捕获表征的时间相似性，忽视了系统故障和潜在异常特征——内部因果关系的破坏。

Method: CAAD框架将外生时序变量建模为残差，通过外生干预导致的显著偏差识别异常；利用多尺度对齐内化系统动态，采用梯度基矩阵监控内部因果关系破坏，量化动态演化和关系拓扑的因果偏差。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，性能优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将异常检测重新定义为格兰杰因果一致性的连续验证，CAAD能够捕捉细微的因果偏移，实现精确的异常检测。

Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [74] [Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08561)
*Dan Yamins,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于解决足够困难任务的任意两个最小深度神经网络，网络表征的弱对齐（基于仿射映射）能保证特权轴的强对齐，且对齐会沿网络层级“拉链式”向上传播，从而在端到端任务优化中涌现出特权轴。这一结果形式化了Cao & Yamins [2024]的逆变性概念，并表明：在足够强的任务驱动下，网络间比较的度量选择并不敏感，趋同演化几乎是必然的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十五年NeuroAI的研究成果引发了两个核心问题：如何将深度神经网络模型与大脑进行比较，以及人工网络与真实大脑网络之间预期存在多大程度的趋同演化。本文旨在从理论上回答这些问题。

Method: 通过理论证明，对任何两个解决足够困难任务的最小DNN解决方案进行分析，建立弱对齐（仿射映射）与强对齐（特权轴）之间的逻辑关系，并揭示对齐沿网络层级传播的机制。

Result: (i) 弱对齐保证特权轴的强对齐；(ii) 对齐沿网络层级“拉链式”向上传播，特权轴从端到端任务优化中涌现。形式化了逆变性概念。

Conclusion: 在足够强的任务条件下，网络间比较的度量选择并不敏感，趋同演化是不可避免的。这对NeuroAI理论具有重要启示。

Abstract: A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.

</details>


### [75] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对对抗性鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，通过考虑最坏情况下的未建模扰动来增强决策稳定性，实验表明该方法相比传统方法能产生更稳定可靠的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策感知实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设模型设定正确，且隐式依赖最优决策在真实扰动下的稳定性，但实际中隐藏或弱建模效应会显著改变决策最优性，导致误导性结论。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论，形式化了对抗性鲁棒的最优决策，并推导了原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则显式针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性。

Result: 在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计会快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而鲁棒感知方法在对抗性变化下产生显著更稳定可靠的决策。

Conclusion: 提出了一种对抗性鲁棒的决策感知实验设计框架，通过考虑最坏情况扰动来增强决策稳定性，为科学实验设计提供了更可靠的决策支持方法。

Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [76] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于偏依赖（partial dependence）的解释引导学习方法，通过调整神经网络使其对特定特征的平均响应符合先验领域知识，在回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上比未约束模型表现更好、数据效率更高，且解释结果与用户知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究多关注开发解释技术或评估解释质量，极少关注如何调整模型使其产生符合先验知识的解释（即解释引导学习）；且现有方法主要针对分类问题，通常假设已知哪些输入特征或区域最重要。

Method: 提出基于偏依赖的解释引导学习方法，在训练过程中约束神经网络对特定特征的**平均响应**（partial dependence），使其与用户指定的函数先验知识对齐。

Result: 在多个回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上，该方法训练的模型比未约束模型性能更好、数据效率更高；且约束模型的解释结果与用户提供的先验知识一致，而未约束模型则不一致。

Conclusion: 本文提出的偏依赖引导训练方法能有效将领域知识注入神经网络，提升回归任务的性能和可解释性，拓展了解释引导学习在回归问题上的应用。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [77] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出了一种无码本的二元球面编码框架，用于大语言模型的极端低位权重复现。该方法通过将权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为球面编码、残差BSQ阶段编码重建误差、以及分类恢复蒸馏三个核心组件，在无需显式码本的情况下实现了高效的极低比特权重复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低位压缩方法面临两难：标量/分组量化在接近2比特/权重时表示能力有限，而向量量化虽提供更丰富的块级表示但引入显式码本、索引查找和额外存储开销。因此需要一种兼顾表示能力和存储效率的极低比特权重复现方法。

Method: 1) 将局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面编码，主载荷为比特打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；2) 残差BSQ阶段编码基础球面编解码器遗留的重建误差，提供无存储码本的显式率-失真路径；3) 分类恢复蒸馏在替换每个Transformer模块类别后进行，减少局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。此外使用8位保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制。

Result: 报告存储预算包括二元码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据，在极低比特条件下实现了有效的权重复现。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本二元球面编码框架有效解决了现有低位压缩方法在极低比特预算下的表示能力与存储效率之间的矛盾，为LLM极低比特权重复现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [78] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了免训练的宽松推测解码技术，统一了现有方法框架，并在当代设置下进行基准测试，得出重要实践结论：宽松解码需要大量的能力评估，且许多方法依赖高质量的辅助模型，不适合轻量级专用多 token 预测草案模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码虽然无损，但近期工作表明放松这一严格保证可以带来进一步加速、可控的能力-速度权衡甚至能力提升。本文旨在系统研究免训练的宽松推测解码技术，为从业者提供实用指导。

Method: 将现有宽松推测解码方法统一到一个共享框架下，在当代设置下进行基准测试，分析不同放松策略的效果和适用条件。

Result: 宽松解码需要相当大的能力评估（与无损推测解码不同）；许多宽松方法依赖草案模型本身是好的语言模型，因此不适合轻量级专用多 token 预测草案模型。

Conclusion: 为从业者提炼了关于宽松推测解码的关键经验教训和实证发现，指出了其适用边界和局限。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [79] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示UMAP内部构建的k近邻图在降维之外具有未被开发的分析潜力，通过PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等标准图算法，可在原始高维空间中进行数据代表性点识别、核心-外围检测和紧邻社区发现，且在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上表现与专用方法相当或互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UMAP的使用主要聚焦在低维嵌入结果，忽略了其内部构建的kNN图蕴含的丰富流形结构信息，该图在降维投影导致的形变之前保留了高维空间的原始数据流形。

Method: 对UMAP内部构建的kNN图应用三种标准图算法：(1) PageRank识别代表性数据点；(2) k-core分解检测稠密核心区域与稀疏外围；(3) 聚类系数发现高度相似数据的紧密邻域。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的定量和定性评估表明，这些图分析结果不仅实用，而且与专用方法（如k-medoids、HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Conclusion: UMAP内部kNN图具备超越降维的额外分析价值，通过标准图算法即可实现多种数据理解任务，为高维数据探索提供了新的视角。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [80] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLORR，一种简单、无状态、保持架构的低秩正则化框架，通过Hoyer稀疏度和核范数的GPU友好近似，在训练中诱导模型压缩性，在ImageNet和LLM预训练中均取得良好效果，且训练开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时低秩正则化方法存在依赖SVD分解、修改模型架构、引入额外可训练参数或依赖有状态缓存等问题，亟需一种简单高效、不改变架构的低秩正则化方法。

Method: 提出SLORR框架，包含基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两种变体，直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似计算前向和后向传播，并提供近似保证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上（ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16的短周期续训及ResNet-18预训练）训练开销低于8%；在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR训练的压缩模型性能显著优于未正则化模型，平均训练开销增加不到1%。

Conclusion: SLORR是一种高效、轻量的低秩正则化方法，能在不改变架构的前提下有效提升模型可压缩性，适用于视觉模型和大语言模型。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


### [81] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在记忆宫殿（LMP）方法，通过自回归潜在空间中的变分推理实现控制策略的适应性推理，在仿真和真实世界任务中取得优异表现，并展现出可解释的自适应计算分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有高度灵活性，能根据任务需求调整思考时间。尽管语言模型已展现出类似的适应性推理能力，但将这种能力迁移到连续控制策略中仍面临挑战，因为直接在语言空间推理缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的粒度。

Method: 将推理建模为具有自回归潜在分布的变分推理，推导出一种潜在空间强化学习技术来可微地优化其变分下界。该方法包含两个变体：LMP-π（控制策略）和LMP-tok（变长动作分词器）。

Result: LMP-π在仿真和真实世界域中均取得强经验性能，展现出可解释的自适应测试时计算分配。LMP-tok作为变长动作分词器，显著提升了下游自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 通过变分推理视角，为控制中的潜在推理提供了新视角，证明了在潜在空间中进行适应性推理的有效性。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [CTA-Pipelining: A Latency-Oriented Spatial Scaling Method for Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07862)
*Tingkai Liu,Muralidhar Andoorveedu,Sanjoy Das,Sanjay Patel,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CTA-pipelining，一种利用共享内存多GPU系统的延迟导向空间扩展执行范式，通过在Cooperative Thread Array级别建立依赖关系实现跨GPU的并发内核执行，在H200和B200系统上相比micro-batching和TP分别降低延迟最多31.8%和29.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多GPU系统虽已演变为紧密集成的共享内存结构，但软件仍将其视为高速网络；同时大语言模型服务对延迟敏感，传统的吞吐导向优化和Tensor Parallelism已不足以满足延迟约束下的扩展需求。

Method: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，在CTA（Cooperative Thread Array）级别建立依赖关系，使依赖内核能够在不同GPU上并发执行，利用共享内存多GPU系统的硬件能力实现延迟导向的空间扩展。

Result: 在8-GPU H200和B200系统上，针对2层GEMM（代表MLP操作），CTA-pipelining相比micro-batching降低延迟31.8%，相比TP降低29.6%；且可与TP正交组合进一步优化延迟。

Conclusion: CTA-pipelining是一种有效的延迟导向空间扩展方法，能充分利用现代共享内存多GPU系统的互连能力，并与现有TP方案正交兼容，为大模型推理场景提供更优的延迟优化方案。

Abstract: The evolution of compute infrastructure has transformed multi-GPU systems into tightly integrated shared-memory structures. However, current software still mostly treats these coherent interconnects simply as high-speed networks. Simultaneously, the demand for serving Large Language Models under latency constraints has shifted GPU workload optimization from being throughput-driven to latency-bound, necessitating latency-oriented scaling methods beyond Tensor Parallelism (TP).
  Thus, we introduce CTA-pipelining, an execution paradigm designed to exploit shared-memory multi-GPU systems. As a latency-oriented spatial scaling technique, CTA-pipelining leverages dependencies at the Cooperative Thread Array level, enabling concurrent execution of dependent kernels across GPUs. We demonstrate its capability using CUTLASS, cuBLAS, and NCCL libraries on 8-GPU H200 and B200 systems. Results show on 2-layer GEMM, representing the MLP operation, CTA-pipelining reduces latency by up to 31.8% compared to micro-batching, and 29.6% compared to TP. It can also be combined with TP as an orthogonal scaling dimension to further push the latency boundary.

</details>


### [83] [Toward a Unified GPU-Aware OpenSHMEM Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08006)
*Naveen Ravi,Nathan Wichmann,Md. Wasi-ur- Rahman,Aurelien Bouteiller,Yıltan Hassan Temuçin,Avinash Kethineedi,Johnathan Alsop,Brandon Potter,Shubhendra Pal Singhal,Jun Shirako,Akihiro Hayashi,Vivek Sarkar,Lawrence C. Stewart,Michael Beebe,Benjamin Michalowicz,Jeongnim Kim,Thiago Teixeria,Mark F. Brown,Aaron Welch,Oscar Hernandez,Wendy Poole,Steve Poole*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一份OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信的辅助规范，通过轻量级、向后兼容的内存模型扩展，为多厂商GPU环境下的RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作提供统一语义，旨在解决现有GPU-aware OpenSHMEM实现碎片化问题，促进社区统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM实现在内存管理、能力发现和操作语义上存在差异，缺乏可移植的加速器架构语义，导致可移植性受限和生态系统碎片化，可能分裂原本旨在统一的OpenSHMEM社区。

Method: 提出一个OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，作为OpenSHMEM 1.x的轻量级向后兼容扩展。通过引入GPU作用域内存空间抽象、能力查询机制以及GPU附加缓冲区在RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作中的明确语义来实现。

Result: 该规范通过主机发起接口的视角初步构想，同时提供通用语义以支持可选的设备发起操作。核心目标是证明GPU-aware OpenSHMEM语义可以在多厂商GPU上实现，为厂商中立规范下的统一提供可行且可快速实施的方案。

Conclusion: 这项工作为未来OpenSHMEM规范的制定提供了参考，是实现多厂商GPU环境下OpenSHMEM统一的关键一步。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems are now accelerator-centric, with GPUs providing most floating-point throughput and memory bandwidth. As next-generation systems increasingly integrate accelerators through high-speed memory fabrics and system interconnects, exposing larger tightly coupled device domains, \ac{PGAS} models such as OpenSHMEM provide a natural abstraction for expressing fine-grained remote memory operations across these devices. While OpenSHMEM 1.x offers a lean PGAS model for irregular communication, atomics, fine-grained synchronization, and collectives, its memory model lacks portable semantics for accelerator architectures. As a result, existing GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM implementations differ in memory management, capability discovery, and operation semantics, limiting portability and ecosystem cohesion. This risks fracturing the community that OpenSHMEM was originally created to unify.
  This paper proposes an OpenSHMEM Auxiliary Specification for GPU-Aware Communication, designed as a lightweight, backward-compatible extension to OpenSHMEM 1.x. The auxiliary specification introduces a minimal memory model extension via a GPU-scoped memory space abstraction, along with capability queries and well-defined semantics for using \acs{GPU}-attached buffers in RMA, atomic, synchronization, and collective operations. This is initially conceived through the lens of a host-initiated interface, although it provides a general set of semantics that also allow for optional device-initiated support. A central goal of this effort is to demonstrate that GPU-aware OpenSHMEM semantics can be specified and implemented across GPUs from multiple vendors, providing a practical and rapidly implementable step toward unification under a vendor-neutral specification while informing the design of future OpenSHMEM specifications.

</details>


### [84] [On the Limitations of Non-GPU AI Accelerators for Large-Model Inference: A Field Study of MoE and Multimodal Serving on Huawei Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08215)
*Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对在华为Ascend 910系统上部署两个大规模推理工作负载进行实地研究，记录了将工作负载从CUDA迁移到非GPU AI加速器（CANN/vLLM-Ascend）时所需的12个源码级补丁、禁用高吞吐特性以保持数值正确性、以及应对设备级故障的操作保障措施，并总结了八大平台局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GPU AI加速器作为大规模模型推理的替代方案日益普及，但将高要求工作负载从CUDA生态迁移到这些平台的实际工程成本缺乏系统记录，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在16设备华为Ascend 910系统上使用CANN和vLLM-Ascend部署两个工作负载（LLM-as-a-judge安全评估管线与多模态医学视觉-语言基准测试），通过源码级补丁、特性禁用和操作保障手段实现可靠运行，并对平台局限性进行分类分析。

Result: 需要12个源码级补丁修复供应商推理插件；需禁用部分高吞吐特性以保证数值正确性；需添加设备级故障操作保障；总结了八类平台局限性：算子与特性支持不完整、并行机制脆弱、底层内核数值错误、图编译不成熟、高级功能不稳定、可扩展性有限、可观测性弱、生态碎片化。

Conclusion: 本文量化了集成工作量、并发行为和基准质量，证明了两个工作负载均能正确服务，为评估或使用非GPU加速器进行大规模模型推理的团队提供了可复现的参考。

Abstract: Non-GPU AI accelerators are increasingly adopted as alternatives to general-purpose GPUs for large-model inference, but the real engineering cost of migrating demanding workloads beyond CUDA remains poorly documented. We present a field study of deploying two large inference workloads on a 16-device Huawei Ascend 910 system using CANN and vLLM-Ascend: an LLM-as-a-judge safety and alignment evaluation pipeline based on a W8A8 MoE judge model, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and a multimodal medical vision--language benchmark based on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision for MMMU and MMMU-Pro. Making these workloads reliable required twelve source-level patches to the vendor inference plugin, disabling several high-throughput features to preserve numerical correctness, and adding operational safeguards for recurring device-level failures. We summarize the main platform limitations in eight categories: incomplete operator and feature support, fragile parallelism, numerical faults in low-level kernels, immature graph compilation, unstable advanced features, limited scalability, weak observability, and ecosystem fragmentation. For each category, we report the symptoms, evidence, and likely causes. We also quantify the integration effort, concurrency behavior, and benchmark quality to show that both workloads were served correctly. Our study provides a reproducible reference for teams evaluating or operating non-GPU accelerators for large-model inference.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Stabilizing Algorithms in the Uniform Port Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08244)
*Liam Brinker,Yuval Emek,Oren Louidor*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"统一端口模型"的分布式计算模型，其中每个图的端口（半边缘）都运行一个恒定大小的有限自动机，实现了真正的均匀性。该模型支持半边缘标签问题（如最大匹配和无源定向），并设计了针对多种局部对称破缺问题（最大独立集、最大匹配、无源定向、最大节点/边k-着色）的高效自稳定算法，这是在真正均匀模型中首次实现此类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以节点为中心的真正均匀分布式计算模型无法自然地表达（半）边标签问题（如最大匹配和无源定向），需要一种新的、真正均匀的模型来支持这类问题，同时探索在弱计算模型下自稳定算法的可能性。

Method: 定义统一端口模型，每个端口（图的半边缘）关联一个恒定大小的局部自动机。基于该模型设计自稳定算法，针对局部对称破缺问题，确保算法在任意初始状态下都能收敛到合法配置。

Result: 在统一端口模型上，针对最大独立集、最大匹配、无源定向和最大节点/边k-着色等基本局部对称破缺问题，设计了多对数运行时间的自稳定算法。

Conclusion: 这是首次在真正均匀的分布式计算模型中证明存在高效的局部对称破缺自稳定算法，扩展了均匀计算模型的能力边界。

Abstract: We introduce a distributed computational model referred to as the \emph{uniform port} model. An algorithm operating in this model is defined by means of local automata associated with the ports (a.k.a.\ half-edges) of the input graph. The crux of the uniform port model is that a single constant-size finite automaton is hosted by every port of every graph, making the model \emph{truly uniform}. Moreover, since the new model explicitly supports the assignment of (input and) output labels to the graph's (half-)edges, it facilitates natural formulations of (half-)edge-labeling problems such as maximal matching and sinkless orientation, which are outside the expressivity scope of prior node-centric truly uniform distributed computational models.
  The main technical contribution of this paper is the design of efficient (i.e., with poly-logarithmic runtime) \emph{self-stabilizing} uniform port algorithms, operating on general graphs, for various fundamental local symmetry breaking problems, including maximal independent set, maximal matching, sinkless orientation, and maximal node/edge $k$-coloring. While efficient self-stabilizing algorithms for local symmetry breaking problems have been extensively studied in stronger computational models, our work is the first to demonstrate the existence of such algorithms in a truly uniform model.

</details>


### [86] [Empirical Analysis of GPU Frequency Behavior Under ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08307)
*Truong-Thanh Le,Hoang-Loc La,Amir Taherkordi,Frank Eliassen,Phuong Hoai Ha,Peiyuan Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时，其动态频率缩放行为会导致内核间存在依赖关系（约80ms窗口内），挑战了现有ML延迟预测技术中“内核延迟独立”的假设，并提出了改进预测模型、内核重排序及NAS驱动设计等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML延迟预测技术普遍假设GPU内核延迟是相互独立的，但实际GPU存在动态频率缩放（DVFS）行为，这一假设可能不成立，导致预测不准确。

Method: 通过在低性能NVIDIA GPU上运行ML/AI工作负载，测量并分析频率缩放行为对内核延迟的影响，观测历史工作负载对当前频率的调制效应（80ms窗口）。

Result: 发现低性能GPU的运行频率显著受到近期（约80ms内）工作负载历史的影响，内核延迟不再独立，存在跨内核的频率依赖性。

Conclusion: GPU动态频率缩放引入的内核间依赖关系违反了独立假设，未来可基于此改进延迟预测模型、开发内核重排序策略以及设计频率/延迟/能耗感知的NAS模型架构。

Abstract: This work presents ongoing research on the frequency scaling behavior of NVIDIA GPUs when executing ML/AI workloads. Our preliminary findings show that, on lower-performance GPUs, the operating frequency is strongly affected by the recent workload history, typically within an 80ms window. This behavior challenges a common assumption underlying several state-of-the-art ML latency-prediction techniques, which treat individual GPU kernel latencies as independent and therefore estimate total execution time by summing isolated per-kernel measurements. Our results indicate that such an assumption does not always hold, as the GPU's dynamic frequency scaling introduces inter-kernel dependencies. We also outline several promising directions for leveraging this observation in future work, including improved latency-prediction models, GPU kernel-reordering strategies, and NAS-driven guidelines for frequency/latency/energy-aware model design.

</details>


### [87] [Adaptive Row Selection Meets Asynchrony in Randomized Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08313)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了异步并行环境下自适应行选择的随机卡奇马尔兹方法，通过在96核节点上的339次实验，揭示了采样激进性与线程数之间的稳定性边界，并提出了实用建议：阈值贪婪选择在高线程数下不稳定，欠松弛可恢复稳定性，不一致读取是更优默认策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应行选择虽能减少迭代次数，但在共享内存的异步并行环境下，工作线程并发修改残差且使用过期数据，其行为尚不明确，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在96核节点上执行339次实验，对残差加权和贪婪卡奇马尔兹方法在异步执行下的表现进行系统测量，使用真实（而非注入的）延迟。

Result: (i) 存在稳定性边界 ℓ*(T)；(ii) 阈值贪婪选择在高线程数下不稳定；(iii) 欠松弛可恢复稳定性；(iv) 一致快照读取存在调度相关的发散问题，而不一致读取从未出现此问题且成本更低。

Conclusion: 不一致读取应作为默认策略；欠松弛提供了可用的安全旋钮；研究结果为分布式两层采样器提供了设计依据。

Abstract: Randomized Kaczmarz is a natural fit for large sparse least-squares and tomographic reconstruction, and adaptive row selection can reduce iteration counts. However, deploying adaptive selection on a shared-memory machine means sampling from a residual that lock-free workers are concurrently modifying, often using stale data. We present the first systematic study of this regime: residual-weighted and greedy Kaczmarz under asynchronous execution, measured across 339 runs on a 96-core node with realized (not injected) delays. Four findings carry directly to practice. (i) Stability is governed by a boundary $\ell^*(T)$ between sampling aggressiveness and thread count; below it, more aggressive sampling is strictly better, so one should tune to just inside the cliff. (ii) Threshold-greedy selection (the standard accelerated rule) is unstable at high thread counts, diverging almost immediately. (iii) Under-relaxation buys back the cliff at a predictable cost, giving a usable safety knob. (iv) Consistent-snapshot reads admit a rare, scheduling-dependent divergence that live (inconsistent) reads never exhibited and that is also cheaper, making inconsistent reads the right default. We validate the implementation against published sequential results and outline the distributed two-level sampler these measurements motivate.

</details>


### [88] [Computing in Anonymous Dynamic Networks with One-Bit Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08358)
*Thibaut Blanc,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了匿名动态网络中每位代理每轮仅广播1比特、仅接收邻居中每种比特值的计数这一极严格限制下的确定性计算问题，证明了在该限制下仍可完成丰富的全局计算，给出了匹配上下界，并展示了单比特通信与O(log n)比特通信在计算能力上几乎等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极端受限通信模型（每轮每节点仅广播1比特，仅知邻居中各比特值的计数）下，匿名动态网络能否完成有意义的全局计算，以及其计算能力与更丰富的通信模型（如拥塞模型）之间的差距。

Method: 提出从局部单比特聚合观测中提取全局线性方程的方法；设计单比特割测试以获取不可区分代理类的守恒约束；通过细化类并收集独立约束来恢复输入多重集的多样性；针对未知网络规模引入自适应洪泛原语。

Result: 有唯一领导者和已知上界U时，可在O(n³log²n + U)轮内终止计算任意可计算函数；无先验知识时，可在O(n³log²n)轮内实现自稳定计算；下界为Ω(n²log(N/n)/log n)轮，当N=2^{Ω(nlogn)}时退化为Ω(n³)轮，且下界即使在唯一领导者、已知n和N、动态环图限制下仍成立。

Conclusion: 拥塞模型下匿名动态网络的计算能力在将每条消息压缩为1比特后几乎得以保持，证明了单比特广播模型与O(log n)比特消息模型在计算能力上本质等价。

Abstract: We initiate the study of deterministic computation in anonymous dynamic networks where each agent broadcasts one bit per round and receives only the number of neighbors broadcasting each bit value. Despite this severe restriction, surprisingly rich global computation is possible.
  With a unique leader and a known upper bound $U$ on the network size $n$, we give a terminating algorithm for any computable function of the input multiset in $O(n^3\log^2 n+U)$ rounds, for inputs from a universe of size $N=2^{O(n\log n)}$. Without prior knowledge of $n$, we design a stabilizing algorithm for the same task running in $O(n^3\log^2 n)$ rounds. This essentially matches the state of the art for the congested model, where messages carry $O(\log n)$ bits and general computation takes $O(n^3)$ rounds. We also obtain comparable results for leaderless and multi-leader networks.
  We complement the upper bounds with an almost-matching lower bound of $$Ω!\left(\frac{n^2\log(N/n)}{\log n}\right)$$ rounds, which becomes $Ω(n^3)$ for $N=2^{Ω(n\log n)}$. The proof is information-theoretic, based on local histories, and holds even with a unique leader, known $n$ and $N$, and a communication graph restricted to a dynamically changing ring.
  Our algorithms extract global linear equations from local one-bit aggregate observations. A one-bit cut test yields conservation constraints on the sizes of indistinguishable agent classes; by refining these classes and collecting independent constraints, agents recover the required multiplicities. For unknown size, we introduce a self-correcting adaptive flooding primitive of independent interest. Thus, the computational power of congested anonymous dynamic networks is essentially preserved even when every message is compressed to one bit.

</details>


### [89] [Coded Task Offloading for Fluid Computing: A Privacy-Aware Approach under D2D Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08440)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向D2D网络的编码任务卸载方案，结合线性秘密共享对任务进行编码分片，在随机任务到达和队列动态下实现阈值恢复、拖后腿缓解与隐私保护，并联合优化时延、能耗与隐私泄露。实验表明该方案优于传统卸载策略，揭示了时延-能耗-隐私三者间的内在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务卸载方案很少考虑对抗性执行环境下的隐私风险和信息泄露问题；同时，大多数编码计算方案仅关注拖后腿缓解，而未考虑能耗感知等系统级目标。因此，亟需一种能同时兼顾隐私保护、能耗优化和性能保障的任务卸载机制。

Method: 将任务卸载技术与线性秘密共享方案相结合，将任务编码为冗余分片以支持阈值恢复、拖后腿缓解和隐私保护；构建隐私感知卸载问题，在含噪泄露观测下联合优化时延与能耗并对理论隐私泄露进行惩罚；使用分支定界求解器与轻量级启发式调度器求解，并通过离散事件模拟器评估。

Result: 编码卸载相比经典的全卸载和并行卸载方案改善了时延-能耗权衡；启发式算法达到近似最优性能，优于基线方法和现有最优求解器；隐私泄露惩罚会重塑卸载决策，暴露出时延-能耗-隐私三者之间的内在权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的编码任务卸载方案能有效兼顾隐私保护与系统性能，在D2D网络中实现了时延、能耗和隐私三者之间的优化平衡，为流体计算环境下的安全高效任务执行提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Fluid Computing aims to support distributed applications execution across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and device resources, motivating task execution mechanisms that adapt to dynamic and privacy-sensitive environments under runtime conditions. In this context, current task offloading schemes rarely address privacy risks and information leakage under adversarial execution settings; furthermore, most coded computing proposals focus on straggler mitigation without considering system-level objectives such as energy awareness. This paper proposes a coded task offloading scheme for D2D networks under stochastic task arrivals and queue-based dynamics. The proposal combines task offloading techniques with linear secret sharing schemes, where tasks are encoded into redundant shares to support threshold-based recovery, straggler mitigation, and privacy preservation while enhancing system performance. Then, we formulate a privacy-aware offloading problem that jointly optimizes delay and energy while penalizing the theoretical privacy leakage of coded tasks under noisy leakage observations. The problem is solved using a branch-and-bound solver alongside a lightweight heuristic scheduler, both of which are evaluated through a discrete-event simulator. Results show that coded offloading improves the delay--energy trade-off with respect to classical full and parallel offloading schemes, while the heuristic achieves near-optimal performance, outperforming baseline and state-of-the-art solvers. The results also show how privacy leakage penalties reshape offloading decisions, exposing an inherent delay--energy--privacy trade-off.

</details>


### [90] [SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08565)
*Jiahao Wang,Kaizhan Lin,Kaixi Zhang,Jinbo Han,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Chenguang Fang,Wenyuan Yu,Rong Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对智能体场景下的LLM请求调度问题进行了系统研究，分析了现有调度器在KV$重用与负载均衡之间的权衡困境，提出了基于会话感知的均衡调度方法SMETRIC，通过差异化调度会话首个请求与后续请求，在保持较高KV$重用的同时实现集群负载均衡，最终在多种部署架构下提升集群TPS达10-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度器主要针对人类对话场景设计，而智能体（Agent）发出的请求具有两大不同特征：(1) 智能体仅关注完整响应而非逐token延迟，使得集群TPS成为首要目标；(2) 请求间KV$重用率极高（生产环境中超过80% vs 聊天场景的54-62%）。现有调度器为最大化KV$重用而过度优先将请求路由到缓存了其KV$的实例上，导致部分实例过载而其他实例空闲，限制了整体TPS。

Method: SMETRIC提出基于会话感知的均衡调度策略：将每个智能体会话中的首个请求纯粹以负载均衡为目标进行路由，而后续请求则以缓存感知方式路由。利用会话轮次信息（session turn）作为调度指标，该信息可从用户输入高效且准确地获取，使调度器保持无状态和简洁。该方法结合了全局KV$存储和本地实例缓存的两级架构优势。

Result: 在预填-解码同部署+全局存储架构下，SMETRIC相比最先进调度器提升集群TPS 10-16%；在预填-解码分离架构下提升预填TPS 2-34%，同时改善了每token延迟。

Conclusion: 通过平衡少量请求（每个会话的首个请求）即可在不牺牲大部分本地KV$重用的前提下实现集群负载均衡，这一发现挑战了"负载均衡必然牺牲KV$重用"的传统认知。

Abstract: LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat.
  This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances.
  SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于上下文图谱（Context Graph）的主动式RAG框架，通过持续监控企业实体状态变化、计算主动性评分（Proactivity Score）并利用LLM进行可解释通知，实现了从被动响应到主动推送的转变。在合同管理、工程事件响应和销售管道三个场景中，Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均发现时间从47分钟降至30秒内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和智能体系统本质上是被动的——它们只能等待用户查询后才会行动。论文认为，真正的企业生产力提升需要主动推送相关、可操作的信息给工作者，而非等待他们提问。

Method: 构建上下文图谱（Context Graph）作为实时关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系和状态变迁；在此基础上设计增量检测引擎（Delta Detection Engine）持续监控状态变化、主动性评分器（Proactivity Scorer）根据紧迫性、相关性和角色匹配度排序候选洞察、以及基于LLM的表层层（Surfacing Layer）生成带可解释理由的排序通知。定义了统一的Proactivity Score函数，并基于NetworkX和Anthropic Claude API提供了完整端到端Python实现。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道卫生三个企业案例中，Precision@5达到0.83，假阳性率为0.11，平均发现时间从反应式基线的47分钟降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 上下文图谱驱动的主动式方法在准确率、低误报率和响应速度上显著优于传统反应式基线，是提升企业生产力的有效范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [92] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI
